Survey packet 128k: The following is a chronological collection of routine field notes. Individual archive records are embedded among unrelated operational observations.

The notes cover maintenance, visitor flow, weather, and record handling at several public sites. Values in ordinary entries are not archive verification codes.

Archive record ORIGIN: This is the opening record in the dispatch packet. At the Alder Quay archive desk, the sealed register lists verification code IRIS-4Q7-LANTERN. Preserve the code exactly as written.

Field entry 00000: On Thursday at 09:50, the site steward visited the east pump house and checked the sample labels against the routine sheet. The observer noted two delivery carts waiting beside the painted line; the comparison read 88 units, within the ordinary range recorded for that location. The crew kept the original notation so later visitors could compare conditions.

Field entry 00001: On Tuesday at 07:10, a maintenance technician visited the west greenhouse and checked the sample labels against the routine sheet. The observer noted a short delay while the key holder finished another inspection; the comparison read 64 units, within the ordinary range recorded for that location. The coordinator asked the next shift to use the same reference point.

Field entry 00002: On Sunday at 17:00, the site steward visited the east pump house and checked the weather display against the routine sheet. The observer noted a steady reading after the instrument was allowed to settle; the comparison read 79 units, within the ordinary range recorded for that location. The summary was filed with the other ordinary site notes.

Field entry 00003: On Monday at 17:10, the site steward visited the market service road and checked the inspection ledger against the routine sheet. The observer noted a brief change in the wind after the clouds moved east; the comparison read 86 units, within the ordinary range recorded for that location. The note was copied into the routine log before the shift changed.
